Legend line 1: gene class (R ribosomal protein: Ribosomal_* Pfam or KO ribosomal protein; N literature member;
X other; U no Pfam and no KO), UPPER CASE inside the run span; junction mark after each gene: ' ' linked
(same strand, operon spacing), '/' not linked (distance or X-cut rule), '|' strand change; '|' at the ends =
contig end. Line 2 strand. Line 3 T = accurate target locus (C or components). Line 4 output loci: B = the
output's best locus for the run, o = other loci. Junction notes: grp a/b = other genomes of the own group
with the same adjacency / holding both genes; othergrp = other groups with the adjacency; bond = this
output's s_k (reported only).
